- **headline-variant-scorecard**: Writes ten headline variants for an article summary, two each from five named formulas (number, how-to, question, contrast, specific benefit), counts characters and words, scores each variant 0-2 on specificity, promise, clarity, accuracy and length, and picks the top two. Use when a writer has an article summary and wants to compare headline options against explicit criteria instead of keeping the first one. Do not use for writing the article body, keyword research, social post copy, or headlines for content that was not supplied.
